# Archived Container Runtime releases

Container Runtime versions 2.2 through 2.9 will continue to receive patches for
known exploitable critical vulnerabilities until March 1, 2027. After that date,
they move to full End of Support: Snowflake no longer applies security patches,
and workloads that use these versions aren’t eligible for technical support.
Snowflake recommends upgrading workloads from those versions to Stable version
2.10 or to Latest.

To change the runtime on an existing service, see [Using a runtime](/developer-guide/container-runtime/using-runtime).
For Container Runtime 3, see [Version 3](/developer-guide/container-runtime/version-3).
For Container Runtime 2.10, see [Version 2](/developer-guide/container-runtime/version-2).

Unless a section lists an additional notable change, each of these releases includes
operating system updates, system library upgrades, and Python package upgrades.

## Release index

The following table lists the archived 2.x releases, newest first. Each version
shipped Python 3.10, 3.11, and 3.12:

| Version | GA date | Python | Package lists |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| [2.9](#label-container-runtime-2-9) | 2026-08-24 | 3.10, 3.11, 3.12 | [CPU](#label-container-runtime-2-9-cpu-packages), [GPU](#label-container-runtime-2-9-gpu-packages) |
| [2.8](#label-container-runtime-2-8) | 2026-07-27 | 3.10, 3.11, 3.12 | [CPU](#label-container-runtime-2-8-cpu-packages), [GPU](#label-container-runtime-2-8-gpu-packages) |
| [2.7](#label-container-runtime-2-7) | 2026-06-29 | 3.10, 3.11, 3.12 | [CPU](#label-container-runtime-2-7-cpu-packages), [GPU](#label-container-runtime-2-7-gpu-packages) |
| [2.6](#label-container-runtime-2-6) | 2026-05-25 | 3.10, 3.11, 3.12 | [CPU](#label-container-runtime-2-6-cpu-packages), [GPU](#label-container-runtime-2-6-gpu-packages) |
| [2.5](#label-container-runtime-2-5) | 2026-04-16 | 3.10, 3.11, 3.12 | [CPU](#label-container-runtime-2-5-cpu-packages), [GPU](#label-container-runtime-2-5-gpu-packages) |
| [2.4](#label-container-runtime-2-4) | 2026-04-03 | 3.10, 3.11, 3.12 | [CPU](#label-container-runtime-2-4-cpu-packages), [GPU](#label-container-runtime-2-4-gpu-packages) |
| [2.3](#label-container-runtime-2-3) | 2026-02-26 | 3.10, 3.11, 3.12 | [CPU](#label-container-runtime-2-3-cpu-packages), [GPU](#label-container-runtime-2-3-gpu-packages) |
| [2.2](#label-container-runtime-2-2) | 2026-01-19 | 3.10, 3.11, 3.12 | [CPU](#label-container-runtime-2-2-cpu-packages), [GPU](#label-container-runtime-2-2-gpu-packages) |

## 2.9 (2026-08-24)

### System environment

| Artifact | Version |
| --- | --- |
| Base image | Ubuntu 24.04 |
| Java | OpenJDK 17 |
| Python | 3.10, 3.11, 3.12 |

Expand

Show lessSee more

### Changes

**Added**

- Altair charts render in Workspace notebooks by default. You no longer need
  `alt.renderers.enable("mimetype")`.

**Changed**

- Operating system updates
- System library upgrades
- Python package upgrades

## 2.8 (2026-07-27)

### System environment

| Artifact | Version |
| --- | --- |
| Base image | Ubuntu 24.04 |
| Java | OpenJDK 17 |
| Python | 3.10, 3.11, 3.12 |

Expand

Show lessSee more

### Changes

**Added**

- Stage sharding for reading many small files from a stage.
- Optional disk cache for repeated stage and table reads. Off by default.

**Changed**

- Training widgets are on by default in Workspace notebooks.
- Distributed PyTorch training always uses the Ray Train path. Checkpoints are written
  to the stage, not local `/tmp`.
- `TunerConfig(uses_snowflake_trainer=...)` is now required. Omitting it raises at
  construction.
- Hyperparameter tuning can run on the driver instead of as a Ray Job when
  `TUNE_DISABLE_RAY_JOB=true`. The Ray Job path remains the default.
- Operating system updates
- System library upgrades
- Python package upgrades

**Deprecated**

- `get_context().get_model_dir()` and `get_context().get_artifact_manager()`. Persist
  models with Ray Train checkpointing instead.

**Fixed**

- Named-stage `artifact_stage_location` values without a leading `@` no longer fail
  Ray Train storage.

## 2.7 (2026-06-29)

### System environment

| Artifact | Version |
| --- | --- |
| Base image | Ubuntu 24.04 |
| Java | OpenJDK 17 |
| Python | 3.10, 3.11, 3.12 |

Expand

Show lessSee more

### Changes

**Added**

- Added support for ipywidgets in notebooks.

**Changed**

- Distributed PyTorch training defaults to the Ray Train path. Set
  `PYTORCH_USE_LEGACY_TRAINER=1` to use the legacy trainer.
- `TunerConfig` defaults `resource_per_trial` to `{"CPU": 1}` when
  `uses_snowflake_trainer=True`, because the inner distributed trainer creates
  its own placement group for training workers.
- Operating system updates
- System library upgrades
- Python package upgrades

**Fixed**

- Fixed `get_session()` overwriting the notebook database and schema context.

## 2.6 (2026-05-25)

### System environment

| Artifact | Version |
| --- | --- |
| Base image | Ubuntu 24.04 |
| Java | OpenJDK 17 |
| Python | 3.10, 3.11, 3.12 |

Expand

Show lessSee more

### Changes

**Added**

- Optional Ray Train backend for distributed PyTorch training. Opt in with
  `PYTORCH_USE_LEGACY_TRAINER=0`. The legacy trainer remains the default
  (`PYTORCH_USE_LEGACY_TRAINER=1`) in this release.
- `train_func` may optionally accept a `config` dict argument. Zero-argument
  functions continue to work.

**Changed**

- Opting into the Ray Train PyTorch backend (`PYTORCH_USE_LEGACY_TRAINER=0`)
  differs from the legacy trainer:
  - Ray Train calls `init_process_group()` automatically. Guard user calls with
    `if not torch.distributed.is_initialized()` and remove or guard
    `destroy_process_group()`.
  - `trainer.run(use_ray_job=...)` is ignored. Training always runs in the
    driver process.
  - `ray.train.torch.prepare_data_loader()` auto-moves batches to GPU. Pass
    `move_to_device=False` or create intermediate tensors on the target device.
- Notebook references to SQL cell results now store as Snowpark DataFrames by default
  instead of pandas DataFrames. This mitigates kernel out-of-memory errors from large
  query results but changes the variable type. Code that uses pandas-specific APIs
  (`.iloc`, `.shape`, `len()`, `.iterrows()`, `.plot()`, `.to_dict()`) on SQL cell
  reference variables must either call `.to_pandas()` to materialize the result first,
  or set `%config SQLMagic.sql_result_type = "pandas"` at the top of the notebook to
  restore the previous behavior. For more information, see
  [Referencing SQL cell results](/user-guide/ui-snowsight/notebooks-in-workspaces/notebooks-in-workspaces-edit-run#referencing-sql-cell-results).
- Operating system updates
- System library upgrades
- Python package upgrades

**Deprecated**

- Legacy custom PyTorch trainer (`PYTORCH_USE_LEGACY_TRAINER=1`). Still the default
  in this release.
- `get_context().get_metrics_reporter()`. Use `ray.train.report({"loss": ...})`
  instead.
- `get_context().get_rank()`. Use `get_world_rank()`.
- `get_context().get_master_addr()` / `get_master_port()`. Read
  `os.environ["MASTER_ADDR"]` / `os.environ["MASTER_PORT"]` instead.
- `get_context().get_default_backend()` / `get_supported_backends()`. Use
  `torch.distributed.get_backend()` after process group initialization.

## 2.5 (2026-04-16)

### System environment

| Artifact | Version |
| --- | --- |
| Base image | Ubuntu 24.04 |
| Java | OpenJDK 17 |
| Python | 3.10, 3.11, 3.12 |

Expand

Show lessSee more

### Changes

**Added**

- Added the `%notebook_exit` magic so non-interactive and scheduled notebooks can
  exit early.

**Changed**

- Operating system updates
- System library upgrades
- Python package upgrades
